Motivation: 现有的LVLM检索器直接编码多模态输入，容易忽略细粒度判别线索，导致语义相似候选项混淆。已有的CoT方法仅基于查询本身生成推理，解释了查询描述什么而非检索器误解了什么，因此需要构建基于检索反馈的推理来真正改善检索性能。

Method: 提出UniME-R1框架，包含adviser模块分析初始检索到的候选，识别embedder混淆的判别线索并生成RC-CoT。若目标在top-k中则直接重排；否则使用双模式embedder结合RC-CoT进行全库重检索。训练上通过挖掘难负样本模拟检索失败，联合优化直接检索与RC-CoT增强检索，并用监督学习和检索导向强化学习对齐adviser。

Result: 在MMEB-V2及多个通用多模态检索基准上，UniME-R1一致地超过了强基线，验证了检索反馈条件化推理的有效性。

Conclusion: 有效的检索推理应基于检索反馈而非仅依赖查询本身，UniME-R1通过adviser生成检索中心化CoT显著提升了统一多模态检索性能。

Abstract: Unified multimodal retrieval aims to identify candidates that satisfy complex user intent expressed through heterogeneous inputs. Although Large Vision-Language Model (LVLM)-based retrievers are efficient and scalable, directly encoding raw multimodal inputs often misses fine-grained discriminative cues, leading to confusion among semantically similar candidates. Recent methods mitigate this limitation by generating Chain-of-Thought (CoT) rationales to enrich the query representation. However, such reasoning is typically derived from the query alone: it explains what the query describes, but not what the retriever misunderstands. We argue that effective retrieval reasoning should instead be conditioned on retrieval feedback. Based on this insight, we introduce UniME-R1, an embedder-adviser framework that learns to reason over initially retrieved candidates and generate Retrieval-Centric Chain-of-Thought (RC-CoT). The adviser analyzes candidates individually to identify the discriminative cues confused by the embedder. If the target appears in the initial top-k set, UniME-R1 directly reranks the candidates; otherwise, it generates RC-CoT to refine the retrieval direction and performs full-corpus re-retrieval with a dual-mode embedder. To train the framework, we mine hard negatives to simulate realistic retrieval failures, jointly optimize direct retrieval and RC-CoT-augmented retrieval, and align the adviser with retrieval outcomes through supervised learning and retrieval-oriented reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments on MMEB-V2 and a diverse set of general multimodal retrieval benchmarks demonstrate that UniME-R1 consistently improves retrieval performance over strong baselines.

</details>


### [69] [Bar-JEPA: Extracting Values from Bar Chart with Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06062)
*Poonam Poonam,Alexander Epple,Timo Ropinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Bar-JEPA，一个用于条形图数值恢复的管线：使用JEPA编码器以自监督方式提取语义丰富的潜在特征，再由一个简单快速的解码器输出刻度与条形坐标，从而恢复条形数值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注图表反渲染的真实世界数据稀缺且标注耗时，而传统的端到端监督方法对数据效率低。JEPA架构能在自监督下学习高语义质量的特征，因此作者希望利用JEPA提升条形图数值恢复的性能和数据效率。

Method: 管线包含两个阶段：1) JEPA编码器通过自监督联合嵌入预测产生高语义质量的潜在特征；2) 一个简单、训练快的解码器消费这些特征，输出刻度（ticks）和条形（bars）的坐标，利用这些坐标恢复条形数值。同时进行了自监督微调并与端到端监督基线进行对比。

Result: 实验表明，自监督微调的有效性以及JEPA提取的特征质量优于端到端监督基线模型，验证了JEPA特征对下游数值恢复任务的有效性。代码、数据集和checkpoints已在GitHub公开。

Conclusion: JEPA编码器提供的语义丰富特征可显著提升条形图数值恢复的效率和准确度，且解码器简单易训，整个管线具有实用性。

Abstract: Bar charts are commonly used in data visualization, and while they are easily understood by humans, it is non-trivial to extract the underlying data computationally. For a machine-learning-based approach, training chart de-rendering models usually requires labeled, real-world data. Labeling data is a time consuming task, which is why annotated data is scarce. Models can learn more efficiently when provided with features of high semantic quality, which a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) is designed to learn in a self-supervised manner. We present a per-bar, numerical value recovery pipeline for bar charts, where a JEPA encoder is used to produce semantically rich latent features. The decoder model consuming these features is simple and quick to train and outputs the coordinates of ticks and bars, which can be used to recover bar values. The effectiveness of self-supervised finetuning and quality of the extracted features is evident when comparing our model to end-to-end supervised baselines. Code, datasets and checkpoints are available on \href{https://github.com/dralois/Bar-JEPA}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [70] [The Next Screenshot Knows: Gated Hindsight Distillation for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06065)
*Weiwei Li,Junzhuo Liu,Tong Chu,Hengfu Yu,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Gated Hindsight Distillation（GHD），利用下一屏幕截图作为训练时的特权信息，解决GUI智能体在离线模仿学习中因"动作正确性证据只出现在后续屏幕上"而难以学习正确推理的问题。GHD通过参数共享的教师模型基于后见截图对学生模型的在线（on-policy）响应进行重新评分，仅在学生失败且教师能恢复演示动作时施加蒸馏。在AndroidWorld和AndroidLab上，GHD相较GRPO在两种视觉语言模型上均提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GUI智能体训练将每个轨迹分解为前缀-动作对，丢弃后续观察，导致模型无法获得"为什么该动作正确"的证据。由于证据往往只出现在下一屏幕上（例如点击Edit/View才看到Soft Wrap选项），标准模仿学习几乎无法让模型采样到正确推理路径。

Method: 提出Gated Hindsight Distillation：学生模型仅从可观察轨迹前缀预测动作，教师模型额外观察下一截图（特权信息）并重新评分学生的在线响应。采用门控机制，仅在学生失败且后见条件教师能恢复演示动作时施加蒸馏，从而过滤低质量蒸馏信号。

Result: 在AndroidWorld和AndroidLab两个基准上，GHD相比GRPO在所测试的两种视觉语言模型上均取得更高的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 利用后续屏幕作为特权信息进行条件化蒸馏，能够显式地向学生模型注入"动作正确性证据"，缓解离线模仿学习中缺乏推理依据的问题，是一种有效且通用的GUI智能体训练增强方法。

Abstract: GUI agents are commonly trained offline from successful interaction trajectories. Standard training decomposes each trajectory into prefix-action pairs: the agent predicts an action from the current screen and interaction history, while the subsequent observation is discarded. This removes the rationale of why an action is correct: the evidence often appears only on the subsequent screen. For example, to enable Soft Wrap, the agent should click Edit or View, but nothing reveals this until the menu opens. Without such evidence, standard imitation gives the model little chance of ever sampling and thus learning the correct reasoning. To address this issue, we propose Gated Hindsight Distillation (GHD), which uses the next screenshot as privileged information during training. A student predicts from the observable trajectory prefix, while a parameter-sharing teacher additionally observes the next screenshot and re-scores the student's on-policy responses. We apply distillation only when the student fails and the hindsight-conditioned teacher recovers the demonstrated action. GHD improves task success over GRPO on AndroidWorld and AndroidLab across two vision-language models. The code and checkpoints will be made available.

</details>


### [71] [Domain-Grounded Candidate Selection for Agentic Image Editing: A Shadow Removal Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06075)
*Shilin Hu,Jingyi Xu,Dimitris Samaras,Hieu Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究商用视觉语言模型在阴影去除任务上是否取代经典物理先验。发现商业生成编辑器可直接产生高质量无阴影编辑，但存在幻觉和物理错误；通过引入代理候选选择管线，并将阴影形成物理学（光照遮挡而非材质/几何）注入生成器与评估器的提示中，在ShadowRemovalRefine基准上CDD达到0.0075，较最强先验方法降低至少47%。结论是物理先验不因VLM而被取代，反而用于约束和引导物理约束不足的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用视觉语言模型的出现引发了根本性问题：它们是否降低了对经典物理驱动低层视觉先验的需求？阴影去除是研究此问题的理想场景，因为它受场景几何、光照、材质和遮挡物共同塑造，且成对数据难以大规模收集。理解VLM是否及如何取代或需要物理先验，对低层视觉研究方向具有重大意义。

Method: 提出代理候选选择管线：生成编辑器先生成引导式探针，评估器筛选重大失败并进行重试，采样多个候选、过滤后进行最终选择，在去除阴影与场景保持之间取得平衡。核心洞察是将阴影形成物理学（将阴影视为光照遮挡引起的照明效应，而非材质或物体结构）注入生成器和评估器的提示中，从而改善生成质量与一致性。

Result: 在ShadowRemovalRefine基准上，物理导向管线CDD达到0.0075，较最强先前方法降低至少47%。商用编辑器直接使用可产生干净的阴影去除效果且保持表面纹理和局部外观，但存在幻觉物体、将阴影误读为材质/几何等物理错误的新失效模式，代理管线有效缓解了这些问题。

Conclusion: 商用视觉语言模型并未取代经典低层视觉先验；相反，物理先验仍然用于约束和引导物理上约束不足的生成过程。该研究为理解VLM与物理建模在低层视觉中的互补关系提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Commercial vision-language models are reshaping computer vision, with visual priors broad enough to rival task-specific systems. This raises a natural question: do they reduce the need for classic, physics-informed low-level vision? We study this through shadow removal, a problem shaped by scene geometry, illumination, materials, and occluders, where paired shadow and shadow-free data are hard to collect at scale. We find that a commercial generative editor, used directly, can produce clean shadow-free edits that preserve surface texture and local appearance. However, this comes with a new failure mode: the same editor can regenerate scene content, hallucinate objects, or misread a shadow as material or geometry, producing plausible but physically wrong edits. We address this with an agentic candidate-selection pipeline: the editor generates a guided probe, an evaluator screens for major failures, retries when needed, samples multiple candidates, filters them, and selects a final result balancing shadow removal against scene preservation. Grounding this process in shadow-formation physics makes it more reliable: prompting the generator and evaluator to treat shadows as illumination effects caused by light occlusion, not material or object structure, measurably improves quality and consistency. On the ShadowRemovalRefine benchmark, our physics-oriented pipeline achieves a CDD of 0.0075, reducing CDD by at least 47% over the strongest prior method. These results suggest that commercial vision-language models do not replace classic low-level vision priors; instead, such priors remain useful for constraining and steering physically underconstrained generation.

</details>


### [72] [Dense-Cast: A lightweight ensemble of deep learning architectures for precipitation nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06082)
*Gourav Jyoti Kalita,Hidam Kumarjit Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个轻量级深度学习模型用于印度东北部地区半小时级降水临近预报，结合DenseNet、残差连接和Transformer编码器，以过去5个半小时降水数据预测未来两个半小时，在GPM IMERG数据集上达到MAE 0.235mm、RMSE 0.735mm、KGE 0.816。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降水临近预报对灾害管理至关重要，但降水的变异性与非线性使其预测具有挑战性；同时，在时空数据中捕捉时间依赖性是降水临近预报的核心难点。

Method: 融合DenseNet架构、残差连接和Transformer编码器构建轻量级深度学习模型，减少模型参数的同时保持预报能力。模型输入前5个半小时降水时间步，预测接下来两个半小时的降水。

Result: 在30分钟预报间隔下实现最佳MAE 0.235毫米、RMSE 0.735毫米和KGE得分0.816。

Conclusion: 提出的轻量级模型在降低参数量的同时有效实现降水临近预报，验证了DenseNet、残差连接与Transformer结合的可行性。

Abstract: Proper short-term forecasting of precipitation is crucial in disaster management and preparedness. Nonetheless, the variability and nonlinearity of precipitation make short-term forecasting challenging for meteorologists. Moreover, capturing temporal dependencies in spatiotemporal data is a challenge in precipitation nowcasting. In this article, we introduce a lightweight deep learning model for half-hourly precipitation nowcasting. This model has been designed by incorporating the DenseNet architecture, residual connections, and transformer encoders for effective precipitation nowcasting with reduced model parameters. The North-Eastern region of India has been selected as the area of interest for our study. The region receives the highest precipitation during the months of June-September due to the monsoon season. The proposed model takes the previous five time-steps of half-hourly precipitation as inputs and predicts the precipitation in the next two half-hours. The GPM IMERG precipitation dataset with a 30-minute cadence has been used in this study for training and testing the model. The proposed architecture achieves best MAE of 0.235 millimetres, RMSE of 0.735 millimetres, and KGE score of 0.816 at an interval of 30 minutes.

</details>


### [73] [Confidence matters: Leveraging Multi-view Geometric Priors for GS-based Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06117)
*Hongyu Zhou,Zorah Lähner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了将几何先验（预测的法线图与深度图）集成到3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）框架中，以改善重建几何质量。实验表明，多视图预测（如VGGT）优于单视图方案，且多视图模型附带的不确定性置信图可显著提升先验的有效性，在含高镜面物体的复杂场景中获得一致的重建质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS依赖SfM初始化和光度优化，在具有高镜面反射的物体上容易产生次优的几何重建。作者希望引入几何先验（法线、深度预测）来弥补这一缺陷，并系统性地分析不同形式的先验（单视图vs多视图）在GS框架中的效果。

Method: 将预测的法线图和深度图作为几何先验集成到3DGS优化框架中。具体分析了单视图与多视图预测模型（如VGGT，基于视觉几何的变换器）生成的先验效果，并利用多视图模型附带输出的置信图对每个预测进行加权，从而在优化中自适应地利用高置信度预测。

Result: 在标准基准上的广泛实验表明，多视图预测优于单视图替代方案，引入置信度加权可显著提升先验效果，在标准场景中一致改善重建质量，在含镜面物体的复杂场景中获得显著增益。

Conclusion: 多视图几何先验配合置信度加权是提升3DGS几何重建质量的有效途径，尤其对高镜面物体的重建具有明显优势，为后续GS方法的先验注入提供了清晰的设计指导。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a widely-used tool for novel view synthesis, offering real-time rendering in a sparse representation. However, the method's reliance on structure-from-motion initialization and photometric optimization can lead to suboptimal geometric reconstruction, particularly for objects with high specularity. In this work, we investigate the integration of geometric priors, in the form of predicted normal and depth maps, into the 3DGS framework to improve the reconstruction quality. We analyze the effect of incorporating these priors into GS-based methods and our evaluation reveals that multi-view predictions, as they are done by the recent visual geometry grounded transformer (VGGT), outperform single-view alternatives. A major factor is the existence of a confidence map for the estimations, which comes as a by-product of multi-view models and which can significantly improve the effectiveness of priors by weighting each prediction appropriately. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks show consistent improvement in reconstruction quality and significant gains in complex scenes including specular objects.

</details>


### [74] [Sample-Adaptive Latent Rewards for Uncertainty-Guided Diffusion Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06125)
*Rui Li,Yuanzhi Liang,Ke Hao,Ziqiao Weng,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,XueLong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SURE，一个统一的潜在空间框架，用于图像和视频扩散模型对齐。包含SURE-LRM（样本自适应潜在奖励模型，预测高斯分布以同时输出奖励分数和不确定性）和SURE-REFL（不确定性引导的奖励反馈学习，沿去噪轨迹提供稠密反馈）。整个过程在潜空间完成，无需解码到像素空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在奖励模型仅输出标量分数，无法估计预测不确定性，导致生成器无法判断反馈可靠性，可能使优化方向错误并引发奖励欺骗（reward hacking）。

Method: 1) SURE-LRM：对每个噪声潜变量预测高斯效用分布，均值预测奖励分数，方差反映预测不确定性（无需人工标注）。2) SURE-REFL：利用学习到的分布进行不确定性引导的稠密后训练，在选定的去噪转换点查询冻结的SURE-LRM，将分离的方差转换为样本可靠性权重，每个加权奖励仅通过其局部转换反向传播，全程保持潜空间操作。

Result: SURE-LRM在偏好预测上优于强基线；SURE-REFL在多种指标上达到SOTA，提升优化稳定性，并在VBench质量、语义和总分上取得最高分。

Conclusion: 通过不确定性引导，SURE在潜空间中实现了更有效、更稳定的扩散模型对齐，避免了像素空间解码的开销和奖励欺骗问题。

Abstract: Latent reward models can supervise visual diffusion models without decoding intermediate states into pixel space. This makes alignment with human preferences more efficient. However, existing latent reward models output only scalar scores. They do not estimate the uncertainty of each prediction. The generator therefore cannot determine which feedback is reliable. This can drive optimization in the wrong direction and lead to reward hacking. We propose \textsc{SURE}, a unified latent-space framework for image and video diffusion models. It learns reward distributions and directly uses their reliability to guide dense post-training. First, we propose sample-adaptive latent reward model (\textsc{SURE-LRM}). It predicts a Gaussian utility for each noisy latent. Its mean predicts the reward score. Its variance reflect the uncertainty of prediction without human annotation. The learned distribution then guides post-training through uncertainty-guided reward feedback learning (\textsc{SURE-REFL}). This method provides uncertainty-guided dense feedback along the denoising trajectory. At selected transitions, \textsc{SURE-REFL} queries the frozen \textsc{SURE-LRM}. It converts detached variance into reliability weights for samples at the same transition. Each weighted reward is backpropagated only through its local transition. The entire process remains in latent space and requires neither pixel-space decoding nor the full denoising graph. Experiments show that \textsc{SURE-LRM} improves preference prediction over strong baselines. \textsc{SURE-REFL} achieves the sota performance among various metrics and further improves optimization stability. It also achieves the highest VBench quality, semantic, and total scores among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [75] [Patient Pose Assessment Using a CT-Based Framework for Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06126)
*Manuel Laufer,Dominik Mairhöfer,Malte Sieren,Hauke Gerdes,Fabio Leal dos Reis,Arpad Bischof,Thomas Käster,Erhardt Barth,Jörg Barkhausen,Thomas Martinetz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，利用CT扫描合成生成深度图像与X光片配对的训练数据，用于AI自动评估放射摄影中患者的摆位质量。在包含3077对上踝关节图像对的合成数据集上预训练，可将真实上踝关节摆位评估准确率提升最多11个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射影像的诊断质量高度依赖患者摆位，而摆位难以标准化且缺乏自动化评估手段。虽然基于深度图像的AI摆位评估有望解决这一问题，但受监管限制，现实中难以获取足够的深度图像与对应X光片配对训练数据。

Method: 提出一个从CT扫描合成生成训练数据（深度图像+X光片配对）的框架，利用合成数据预训练后，迁移到真实上踝关节的摆位评估任务中。

Result: 在合成的3077对图像对上完成预训练后，真实上踝关节摆位评估准确率提升高达11个百分点。

Conclusion: 合成数据预训练策略有效解决了医疗场景下数据获取困难的问题，为放射摄影前自动摆位评估提供了可行方案。

Abstract: An adequate diagnostic quality of radiographs is essential for reliable diagnoses and treatment planning. The patient's pose during radiography is one of the most important factors determining the diagnostic quality. Since patient positioning is difficult and not standardized, an automated AI-based approach using depth images to automatically assess the patient's pose before the radiograph has been taken would be helpful. Due to regulatory hurdles, however, it is difficult in practice to acquire the required depth images and corresponding radiographs. In this paper, we present a framework that can generate such training data synthetically from Computed Tomography scans. We further show that by pretraining on our generated synthetic dataset consisting of 3077 image pairs of upper ankle joints, the pose assessment of real upper ankle joints can be improved by up to 11 percentage points.

</details>


### [76] [Learning visual representations for compositional analysis of artworks and photographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06142)
*Fatemeh Behrad,Tinne Tuytelaars,Johan Wagemans*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对比了两种构图分析范式：基于人类感知分组的可解释方法（物体中心模型+图注意力网络）与基于大规模数据微调的视觉基础模型，在构图评分/类别预测、构图图像检索和视觉显著性检测三项任务上评估。冻结编码器下人类启发方法表现相当且可解释，但在数据充足时可微调的大规模自监督模型显著占优，代价是牺牲可解释性和跨域泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构图（composition）是艺术作品传达意义、情感与美学质量的核心，但仍是视觉理解中最缺乏形式化的维度。先前工作表明学习有意义的构图表征存在持续差距，归因于语义偏差（semantic bias），并暗示人类启发的感知分组方法可能是关键突破口。

Method: 比较两种平行范式：(1) 人类启发方法——利用物体中心模型进行区域级分解，结合图注意力网络（GAT）捕获元素间的空间关系；(2) 基于最近大型构图数据集微调的基础模型方法。两者均在构图分数/类别预测、构图图像检索和视觉显著性检测三个任务上评估。

Result: 在冻结编码器条件下，人类启发方法达到与基础模型相当的性能且保持可解释性；当获得足够数据支持微调时，大规模自监督模型性能显著超越人类启发方法，但代价是可解释性和跨领域泛化能力下降。

Conclusion: 构图分析需要在可解释性与性能之间做出权衡：数据充足时基础模型占优，但人类启发的感知分组方法在资源受限或需要跨域泛化与可解释性的场景下仍是有效选择。

Abstract: Composition, the deliberate arrangement of visual elements, is central to how meaning, emotion, and aesthetic quality are conveyed in artwork, yet it remains among the least formalized dimensions of visual understanding. Prior work highlights a persistent gap in learning meaningful compositional representations, attributing it to semantic bias and suggesting that human-inspired approaches may be key. We compare two parallel paradigms for composition analysis: a human-inspired method grounded in perceptual grouping, and fine-tuned foundation models enabled by recent large-scale compositional datasets. The human-inspired approach uses object-centric models for region-level decomposition and a graph attention network to capture spatial relationships between elements. Both paradigms are evaluated on composition score/category prediction, compositional image retrieval, and visual saliency detection. With frozen encoders, the human-inspired method achieves competitive performance while remaining interpretable. When sufficient data enables fine-tuning, large self-supervised models outperform significantly, but at the cost of interpretability and cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [77] [BendTwin: Robust Dense-to-Sparse Physical Reconstruction with Bending-Aware Differentiable Spring-Mass Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06164)
*Yixiong Jing,Qi Wang,Lin Chen,Junwei Jiang,Guangming Wang,Haibing Wu,Olaf Wysocki,Wanli Ma,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BendTwin 是一种弯曲感知的可微分弹簧-质量框架，用于从视频中重建可变形物体并预测其未来状态。它在轴向弹簧基础上引入局部表面三元组上的弯曲刚度和阻尼，通过惩罚偏离静止角度来正则化高阶变形，提升力学稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于弹簧-质量的物理驱动重建方法仅依赖轴向弹簧，在物理图粗化时力学欠约束，难以保持稳定的局部变形，限制了可变形物体重建与预测的物理保真度。

Method: BendTwin 在传统轴向弹簧-质量系统基础上，对局部表面三元组引入弯曲刚度和弯曲阻尼，惩罚偏离静止角度的偏差并正则化高阶变形，从而增强力学稳定性，同时保持弹簧-质量系统的简洁性与可微性。

Result: 实验表明 BendTwin 一致优于仅轴向弹簧的 PhysTwin 基线；消融研究证明弯曲约束在不同下采样比例下均能维持系统稳定性并持续改善原 PhysTwin 的表现。

Conclusion: BendTwin 提供了从稀疏视角 RGB-D 视频构建力学忠实数字孪生的有效方案，兼顾了重建精度与物理一致性。

Abstract: Reconstructing objects with mechanical properties from video observations enables physically consistent dynamic prediction, benefiting robotics planning and interaction. Existing spring--mass based physical driven reconstruction approaches offer efficient and differentiable physical reconstruction, but they typically rely on axial springs alone. Such formulations oversimplify the underlying structural mechanics and can become mechanically under-constrained when the physical graph is coarsened, limiting their ability to preserve stable local deformation. We present BendTwin, a bending-aware differentiable spring--mass framework for video-based reconstruction and future prediction of deformable objects. BendTwin introduces bending stiffness and damping over local surface triplets, penalizing deviations from rest angles and regularizing higher-order deformation. These bending constraints improve mechanical stability while preserving the simplicity of spring--mass system. Experiments show that BendTwin consistently outperforms the axial-only PhysTwin baseline. Ablation studies further demonstrate that the bending constraints maintain system stability across different downsampling ratios and consistently improve upon the original PhysTwin formulation. Overall, BendTwin provides an effective approach for constructing mechanically faithful digital twins from sparse-view RGB-D videos.

</details>


### [78] [Support Operation Factorization: Compositional Readout of Frozen Vision Encoders under Controlled Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06174)
*Zhongyao Wang,Wanli Ouyang,Taoyong Cui,Pheng Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种组合性分析冻结视觉编码器的新方法：SO-OPF 读出器与"留一格"（leave-one-cell-out）注入对齐协议，将细胞能量分解为支持显著性与竞争式操作后验，以区分"已知网格下组合已见绑定"与"从扁平标签恢复网格"两个问题。在 DINOv3 和 SigLIP2 上验证，发现已知因子分配可达高精度，但从扁平标签学习分配存在渲染器相关的失败边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准因子探针分别打分各轴，无法检测多个操作复用同一预测槽位的"操作洗涤"（operation laundering）失败，导致聚合分数会混淆两个本质不同的问题：载体是否真正组合了已见绑定，以及网格是否能从扁平标签回收。

Method: 提出（1）注入对齐的留一细胞协议，在支持×操作网格上进行评估；（2）SO-OPF 读出器，将细胞能量分解为支持显著性和竞争式操作后验。在 Shapes3D-Extended、COCO 及重建的 MuJoCo 底物上，使用冻结 DINOv3 和 SigLIP2 特征进行实验。

Result: 已知因子分配时 DINOv3 在 Shapes3D-Extended 达 0.874 注入精度、COCO 达 0.799；从扁平标签学习时分别为 0.769 和 0.762。匹配轴感知监督下，因式化载体将学习分配精度从 0.653 提升至 0.841 并无洗涤差距。但 MuJoCo 重建底物上 DINOv3 仅为 0.569、SigLIP2 为 0.484，且存在槽位坍缩，表明恢复能力存在渲染器相关边界。

Conclusion: 因式化读出与注入式评估能在两个底物上恢复已见绑定，并暴露而非掩盖渲染器特有的失败边界；但并未建立从扁平标签进行通用恢复的能力。

Abstract: Compositional analysis of frozen vision encoders should determine both what changed and where it changed. Standard factor probes score these axes separately, however, and can reward multiple operations that reuse the same predicted slot. We call this failure operation laundering. We introduce an injectively aligned leave-one-cell-out protocol over support x operation grids and SO-OPF, a readout that factors cell energy into support salience and a competitive operation posterior. This formulation separates two questions that aggregate scores conflate: whether the carrier composes held-out bindings when the grid is known, and whether that grid can be recovered from flat cell labels. With frozen DINOv3 features, known factorial assignment reaches 0.874 injective accuracy on Shapes3D-Extended and 0.799 on globally image-disjoint COCO; learning the assignment from flat labels reaches 0.769 and 0.762, respectively. Under matched-axis-aware supervision on Shapes3D, the factored carrier improves learned-assignment accuracy from 0.653 to 0.841 over a dense carrier and eliminates its laundering gap. SigLIP2 replicates the COCO separation. A rebuilt MuJoCo substrate exposes a boundary: learned-assignment accuracy is 0.569 with DINOv3 and 0.484 with SigLIP2, with substantial slot collapse. Thus factored readout and injective evaluation recover held-out bindings on two substrates while exposing, rather than hiding, a renderer-specific failure boundary; they do not establish universal recovery from flat labels.

</details>


### [79] [EvReflection: Event-Driven Micro-Dynamics for Reflection Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06184)
*Jiaxiao Wang,Dachun Kai,Huyue Zhu,Quanquan Hu,Zhenyang Xu,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EvReflection，一种利用事件相机捕获微动态来解耦反射层与透射层运动先验，从而突破单帧反射消除中固有歧义的新方法。设计了Micro-Dynamics Decoupler和Parallax-Attention Rectifier，并构建了首个真实世界基准数据集EVR²，在合成与真实基准上均达到SOTA（PSNR分别超过最佳方法1.6dB和1.2dB）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反射消除方法主要依赖单帧静态图像先验，由于反射层与透射层之间存在固有的歧义，导致严重的残差伪影。事件相机能够捕获微动态，揭示两层之间的差分运动，从而打破这一歧义。

Method: 1) Micro-Dynamics Decoupler：从事件流中解耦层特异运动作为先验；2) Parallax-Attention Rectifier：利用这些动态先验引导RGB图像中的反射伪影干净移除；3) 构建视差感知仿真pipeline及首个真实世界基准数据集EVR²。

Result: EvReflection在合成与真实世界基准上均达到最先进性能，PSNR分别超越最佳竞争方法1.6dB和1.2dB。代码、数据集和预训练模型已开源。

Conclusion: 利用事件信号提供的微动态信息可以有效解决反射消除中的层间歧义问题，事件相机作为额外传感模态在图像恢复任务中具有显著潜力。

Abstract: Despite remarkable progress in reflection removal, current methods primarily exploit static image priors from a single frame and still suffer from severe residual artifacts due to the inherent ambiguity between the reflection and transmission layers. In this paper, we propose leveraging event signals to break this ambiguity. By employing event cameras to capture micro-dynamics, we reveal the differential motion between these two layers. We thereby present a novel event-driven reflection removal network, EvReflection, that utilizes these dynamic cues for layer separation. Specifically, we design a Micro-Dynamics Decoupler to disentangle layer-specific motions from event streams as priors, which then guide a Parallax-Attention Rectifier to cleanly remove artifacts from the RGB image. Furthermore, to address data scarcity, we develop a parallax-aware simulation pipeline and construct the EVR$^2$ benchmark dataset, the first real-world dataset for this task. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EvReflection achieves state-of-the-art performance on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks, surpassing the best competing method by more than 1.6 dB and 1.2 dB in PSNR, respectively. The code, dataset, and pre-trained models are available at https://github.com/JiaxiaoWang/EvReflection.

</details>


### [80] [HOPE: Hand-Object Pressure Estimation from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06192)
*Subin Jeon,Byungjun Kim,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HOPE框架，将手部压力估计重新定义为以手为中心的、以单目视频为输入的视频预测问题，直接在手动网格上预测逐顶点的法向压力和接触，统一了不同物体形状和传感器布局的输出空间，并通过跨域标注对齐和顶点锚定的视频Transformer实现裸手视频上的接触与压力联合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的压力估计方法局限于平面表面和单张图像输入，难以推广到包含多样物体的动态手-物交互场景。作者希望突破这一局限，构建一个与物体形状无关、可处理动态视频序列的压力估计框架。

Method: HOPE包含两大核心组件：(1) 将触觉手套压力、平面传感器压力和基于距离的手-物接触标注统一对齐到共享的手顶点空间，使裸手接触数据能够在缺少公制标签时正则化压力学习；(2) 引入顶点锚定的视频Transformer，每个顶点作为持续token，聚合时间维度的视觉特征和手部姿态，并通过接触门控压力头确保无接触时压力为零。

Result: 在OpenTouch、PressureVisionDB和手-物接触基准上验证了HOPE在物体压力、表面压力和接触监督的HOI设置下的有效性。尽管公制压力监督主要来自戴手套的手部视频，HOPE能够泛化到裸手第一人称视角和野外视频，产生超越仅接触或仅平面压力基线的联合接触与压力预测。

Conclusion: HOPE成功地将手部压力估计从静态平面场景推广到动态手-物交互，通过统一的顶点空间和视频级Transformer实现了跨数据域的压力与接触联合预测，为该方向提供了一个新的基准方法。

Abstract: Estimating physical pressure from vision is essential for understanding contact-rich hand-object interaction. However, prior vision-based pressure estimation methods are largely limited to planar surfaces and single image input, making them difficult to apply to dynamic hand-object interaction with diverse objects. We instead formulate pressure estimation as a hand-centric video prediction problem with monocular video as input. This formulation predicts temporally evolving per-vertex normal pressure and contact directly on the hand mesh, yielding a unified output space independent of object shape and sensor layout. Building on this formulation, we propose \textbf{HOPE}, a framework with two key components. First, we lift tactile-glove pressure, planar-sensor pressure, and distance-based hand-object contact annotations into a shared hand vertex space, allowing bare-hand contact data to regularize pressure learning where metric labels are unavailable. Second, we introduce a vertex-anchored video transformer that treats each vertex as a persistent token, aggregates visual features and hand pose over time, and uses a contact-gated pressure head to enforce that pressure vanishes without contact. Experiments on OpenTouch, PressureVisionDB, and hand-object contact benchmarks validate HOPE across object-pressure, surface-pressure, and contact-supervised HOI settings. Despite using metric pressure supervision primarily from gloved-hand videos, HOPE generalizes to bare-hand egocentric and in-the-wild videos, producing joint contact and pressure predictions beyond the scope of contact-only or planar-pressure baselines.

</details>


### [81] [CFGPNet: Cross-Attention-Based Fused Gradient Programmed Network Framework for Multispectral Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06205)
*Nima Hatami,Karim Faez,Saeed Sharifian,Hamidreza Amindavar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CFGPNet是一个跨注意力融合梯度编程网络框架，用于多光谱(RGB-T)目标检测。它通过改进的GELAN主干(集成RepViT风格重参数化块)、CrossCEA跨模态注意力模块和ASAF注意力选择聚合融合网络来增强跨模态交互，并引入可编程梯度辅助分支优化梯度传输。在FLIR、M3FD、LLVIP、VEDAI和MFAD五个多光谱基准上取得一致领先性能，并开源代码与模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-T目标检测方法存在三个核心问题：跨模态交互不足、模态分布差异导致融合不稳定、以及基于重注意力架构的高计算成本。作者旨在设计一个既能增强跨模态特征交互、又能保持计算效率的实用检测框架。

Method: 1) 改进GELAN主干，采用RepViT风格重参数化块在增强特征表示的同时保持计算效率；2) 提出CrossCEA(Cross Computation Efficient Attention)模块增强可见光与热红外分支间的跨模态交互并减少冗余信息传递；3) 设计ASAF(Attention Selection and Aggregation Fusion)网络，结合密集特征聚合与选择性注意力强调生成紧凑判别性融合表示；4) 在每个变体中集成可编程梯度辅助分支改善梯度传递与优化质量；5) 提供三种模型尺度。

Result: 在五个公开基准上取得一致优异表现：FLIR 80.7% mAP50/45.0% mAP50:95，M3FD 89.9%/63.4%，LLVIP 97.8%/68.9%，VEDAI 83.3%/56.9%，MFAD 83.4%/61.8%。展示了跨多样场景、目标尺度和模态平衡下的稳定性能，并提供三种模型规模下的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: CFGPNet被证明是一种有效且实用的RGB-T目标检测解决方案，通过跨注意力机制与可编程梯度优化相结合，在精度和效率之间取得了良好平衡。代码、数据和微调模型均已开源。

Abstract: RGB--T object detection exploits the complementary strengths of visible and infrared imagery, supporting robust perception in low-light, adverse-weather, and complex multi-scale environments. However, existing methods still suffer from insufficient cross-modal interaction, unstable fusion from modality distribution gaps, and the high computational cost of heavy attention-based architectures. To address these issues, CFGPNet is proposed, a Cross-Attention-Based Fused Gradient Programmed Network framework for multispectral object detection. CFGPNet uses an improved GELAN backbone with RepViT-style re-parameterized blocks to strengthen feature representation while preserving computational efficiency. A Cross Computation Efficient Attention (CrossCEA) module is introduced to enhance cross-modal feature interaction and reduce redundant information transfer between visible and thermal branches. To generate compact and discriminative fused representations, an Attention Selection and Aggregation Fusion (ASAF) network combines dense feature aggregation with selective attention-based emphasis. Moreover, a programmable-gradient auxiliary branch is integrated into each CFGPNet variant to improve gradient delivery and optimization quality. Experiments on five public multispectral benchmarks, FLIR, M3FD, LLVIP, VEDAI, and MFAD, demonstrate that CFGPNet achieves strong and consistent performance across diverse scenes, object scales, and modality balances. In particular, the framework attains 80.7% mAP50 / 45.0% mAP50:95 on FLIR, 89.9% / 63.4% on M3FD, and 97.8% / 68.9% on LLVIP. It also reaches 83.3% / 56.9% on VEDAI and 83.4% / 61.8% on MFAD. These results show that CFGPNet is an effective, practical solution offering useful accuracy--efficiency trade-offs across three model scales. The code, data, and fine-tuned models are available at https://github.com/NimaHatami99/CFGPNet.

</details>


### [82] [EmoWorld: A Decoupled Affective Field for Controllable Emotional Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06231)
*Bingyuan Wang,Baistan Zhyldyzbekov,Kunyu Feng,Zeyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EmoWorld在冻结的流匹配视频扩散Transformer中解耦情绪生成的三个维度：全局氛围、语义情感线索和时间推进。通过一次性准备阶段提取层间情感方向和可复用线索库，推理时用VAS/SAS/TAS三种策略分别注入，在Wan2.2上显著提升目标情感对齐度且不更新生成器参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成器将全局氛围、情感语义线索和时间进展纠缠在单一文本条件中，导致情感控制不精确、无法独立调节各维度，影响观者对场景的情绪解读。

Method: 冻结流匹配视频扩散Transformer中的三通道解耦：(1)一次性准备阶段从几何保持的中性/情感编辑全景图中提取层间情感方向和可复用线索库；(2) VAS向隐状态注入氛围方向；(3) SAS隔离可缩放提示残差用于语义线索；(4) TAS在去噪和视频时间上插值端点残差场。

Result: 在Wan2.2上：VAS提升目标情感对齐19%并降低时间波动代理48%；SAS提升目标情感对齐37%并增加检测到的情感线索36%；TAS提升过渡单调性15%。在27个情感类别上进行文本到视频和图像到视频评估，跨多个Video-DiT骨干可移植，支持相机条件合成。

Conclusion: EmoWorld证明无需更新生成器参数即可在冻结Video-DiT中实现情感各维度（氛围/语义/时间）的解耦控制，具备跨骨干可移植性和组合能力，为情感可控视频生成提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Emotion shapes how viewers interpret a scene, yet existing video generators entangle global atmosphere, affect-bearing semantic cues, and temporal progression within a single text condition. We present EmoWorld, a framework that decouples these factors within a frozen flow-matching video diffusion transformer (Video DiT). A one-time preparation stage extracts layer-specific affect directions and a reusable cue library from geometry-preserving neutral and emotion-edited panoramas. At inference, Visual Atmosphere Steering (VAS) injects atmosphere directions into hidden states, Semantic Affective Steering (SAS) isolates a separately scalable prompt residual for semantic cues, and Temporal Affective Steering (TAS) interpolates endpoint residual fields across denoising and video time. On Wan2.2, VAS improves target-emotion alignment by 19% while reducing a temporal-fluctuation proxy by 48%; SAS improves target-emotion alignment by 37% and increases detected affect-bearing cues by 36%; and TAS improves transition monotonicity by 15% over the strongest baseline. EmoWorld is evaluated across 27 emotion categories in text-to-video and image-to-video settings, demonstrates portability across multiple Video-DiT backbones, and supports camera-conditioned composition without updating generator parameters.

</details>


### [83] [Depth-Guided Video Object Counting in Crowded Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06236)
*Yuanjing Xu,Xinyan Liu,Weidong Chen,Zixuan Zou,Linhao Zhang,Zhuangzhe Meng,Antoni B. Chan,Weigang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DG-Det，一个用于拥挤场景下视频目标计数的深度引导检测器。通过RGB-D跨注意力融合深度线索与显式遮挡预测，增强空间理解，并引入统一的去重框架消除跨帧冗余计数。同时发布新的RGB-D视频目标计数数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频目标计数方法仅依赖RGB信息，在拥挤和遮挡场景中判别能力受限，难以根据文本或视觉提示稳健地计数目标类别。

Method: 提出深度引导检测器(DG-Det)，包含多尺度RGB-D跨注意力融合和显式遮挡预测模块；设计统一的去重框架处理后处理阶段的跨帧冗余计数；构建包含深度信息与多类目标的RGB-D视频目标计数数据集。

Result: 与现有基线相比，MAE降低62.01%，RMSE也有一致提升。公开了源代码和数据集。

Conclusion: 深度信息能有效提升拥挤遮挡场景下视频目标计数的准确性，RGB-D融合与遮挡预测是解决该问题的有效途径，统一的去重框架减少了跨帧重复计数。

Abstract: Our primary objective is to advance video object counting in crowded scenes, aiming to robustly count all instances of a target category based on given text or visual prompts. Existing methods rely on RGB information, limiting their discriminative ability in crowded and occluded conditions. To address this, we propose a Depth-Guided Detector (DG-Det) along with a general post-processing pipeline. By integrating depth cues with multi-scale RGB-D cross-attention and explicit occlusion prediction, our method enhances spatial understanding and achieves robust detection in crowded and occluded scenes. Furthermore, we introduce a unified de-duplication framework to eliminate cross-frame redundant counting. To facilitate future research, we also release a new RGB-D Video Object Counting dataset featuring depth information and multiple object categories persequence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves a 62.01\% reduction in MAE compared to existing baselines, and also produces consistent improvements in RMSE. We provide the source code at https://github.com/streamer-AP/DG-Net and the dataset at https://huggingface.co/datasets/aerospace123/RGBD-VideoCount.

</details>


### [84] [PRISM: Distribution-Gated Flow Matching for Controllable Unpaired Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06240)
*Elad Yoshai,Natan T. Shaked*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM是一个无GAN的流匹配框架用于无配对图像到图像翻译，通过学习的逐特征门控替代全局噪声/引导控制，在推理时无需重训即可局部覆盖门控，在五个自然与生物医学基准上取得最佳或接近最佳的FID/KID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的无配对图像翻译器用单一全局噪声或引导值控制保留，无法区分"保留内容"与"改变外观"的特征，导致保留与转换之间的控制粒度不足。

Method: PRISM采用flow-matching框架，核心是学习逐特征门控（gate）：门控的空间先验来自每个源特征到目标特征分布的标准距离，远离目标分布的特征被放开、一致的特征被保留。同一门控同时控制初始化（混合真实源潜在表示与任务匹配的破坏）和ODE积分期间的传输时序。破坏与任务匹配：内容锚定（AdaIN）用于保留结构，部分锚定用于改变结构。推理时可用文本或检测器局部覆盖门控而无需重训。

Result: 在五个基准（AFHQ猫->狗、CelebA-HQ外观翻译、昼夜光照、虚拟染色、乳腺冰冻->永久病理）上，在相同分割协议下，PRISM在四个基准上取得最佳Inception FID和KID，第五个竞争力接近；在组织病理学上核计数比最接近理想值，表明目标真实感与结构保留之间平衡良好。

Conclusion: PRISM通过逐特征门控和任务匹配的破坏策略，实现了更精细的无配对图像翻译控制，兼顾目标风格真实感与源图结构保留，并在自然与生物医学任务上验证了泛化性。

Abstract: Unpaired image-to-image translation must decide, per image, what to change and what to preserve without paired supervision. Many diffusion-based unpaired translators control preservation through a single global noise or guidance value applied across the image, which cannot separate content to keep from appearance to change. We present PRISM, a GAN-free flow-matching framework that replaces this global control with a learned per-feature gate. The gate's spatial prior is derived from each source feature's standardized distance to the target feature distribution, so features far from the target are freed while target-consistent features are preserved. The same gate controls both the initialization, which mixes the real source latent with a task-matched corruption, and the transport timing during Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) integration. The corruption is matched to the task, content-anchored (AdaIN) for structure-preserving translation and partially anchored for structure-changing translation, and the gate can be overridden locally at inference from text or a detector without retraining, preserving important structures of the original image while still generating realistic results. We evaluate PRISM on five natural and biomedical benchmarks (AFHQ cat->dog, CelebA-HQ appearance translation, day->night relighting, virtual staining, and breast frozen->permanent histopathology). Among the evaluated methods under a shared same-split protocol, PRISM attains the best Inception FID and KID on four benchmarks and a competitive result on the fifth, and on histopathology yields the nuclei-count ratio closest to the ideal, supporting a favorable balance between target realism and structural preservation.

</details>


### [85] [Toward Deployable Bangla Sign Language Recognition with Expert-Validated Data and a Lightweight Attention-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06252)
*Saad Ahmed,Md Khalid Syfullaha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RSBdSL38，一个包含10,874张经专家验证的孟加拉手语（BdSL）38种手型识别数据集，并设计了一个仅298K参数的轻量注意力卷积网络，在多个基准上达到96.37%准确率，通过量化可在智能手机上以3.98ms/张实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉聋哑人主要通过孟加拉手语（BdSL）交流，但现有自动BdSL识别系统依赖非专家验证的受控数据集，且使用重型预训练骨干网络，不适合移动设备端部署，难以实际普及。

Method: 构建专家验证的RSBdSL38数据集（38种手势、10,874张图像，来自孟加拉三所特教学校的真实手语者）；设计轻量级注意力卷积网络，包含分组瓶颈残差块、通道与空间注意力、多尺度深度可分离手部特征块、双池化和Swish激活，仅298,470参数，从零训练。

Result: 从零训练达到96.37%准确率；在六个公开BdSL基准上重训达到92.95-98.33%，合并语料97.04%，BdSL-38零样本76.25%；量化后模型仅0.48MB，在商用手机15.5MB内存占用下以3.98ms/张运行；消融验证各架构组件贡献显著。

Conclusion: RSBdSL38数据集与轻量从零训练模型将基准准确率转化为可部署的无障碍方案，以远低于预训练骨干的计算成本实现接近最优的识别性能，数据集、代码和模型均已开源。

Abstract: Deaf and hard-of-hearing people in Bangladesh communicate mainly through Bangla Sign Language (BdSL). Automatic BdSL recognition on personal devices could widen access to education and services. Existing systems use controlled-setting datasets without expert verification and heavyweight pretrained backbones unsuited to on-device use. We introduce RSBdSL38, 10,874 expert-validated images spanning all 38 BdSL hand signs, representing the 51 letters of the Bangla alphabet, recorded from real signers at three special-needs schools across Bangladesh. We propose a lightweight attention based convolutional network of 298,470 parameters, built from grouped bottleneck residual blocks, channel and spatial attention, a multi-scale depthwise hand-feature block, dual pooling, and Swish activations. Trained from scratch, it attains 96.37% accuracy (95.72% +- 0.54% over five seeds), within 1.08 percentage points of the best of nine ImageNet-pretrained efficient architectures under an identical protocol, using 8.5 to 68x fewer parameters and 1.3 to 21.7x fewer MACs. Retrained, it reaches 92.95 to 98.33% on six public BdSL benchmarks, 97.04% on a merged corpus, and 76.25% zero-shot on BdSL-38. Removing any architectural stage costs 7.61 to 89.30 points, against at most 3.17 for the training recipe. Grad-CAM with deletion-insertion and weight-randomization checks confirms that predictions follow the signing hand. A signer-independent split holding out 6 of 36 signers yields 85.18%. Quantized to 0.48 MB, it runs at 3.98 ms per image within a 15.5 MB footprint on a commodity smartphone. Together, RSBdSL38 and our from-scratch model turn benchmark accuracy into deployable accessibility at a fraction of pretrained-backbone cost; dataset, code, and models are released.

</details>


### [86] [MASS: Multiplayer World Models with Authoritative Shared State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06257)
*Ziqi Cai,Siqi Yang,Yimu Wang,Zixian Gao,Yunheng Liu,Shuchen Weng,Erwin Wu,Kaipeng Zhang,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MAS框架，将视频世界模型中的世界状态与视角相关视觉隐藏变量解耦，通过Learned Logic Engine推进全局权威的带类型状态，再由Learned Rendering Engine按需生成一致性视角，实现可扩展的多智能体世界模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在多玩家/多智能体环境中将世界状态与视角相关的视觉潜在变量纠缠在一起，导致冗余计算、跨视角不一致和可扩展性差的问题。

Method: 借鉴多人游戏架构，MAS包含两个核心组件：(1) Learned Logic Engine——无需任何手写转移函数，从联合动作中推进全局权威的带类型状态，作为唯一的循环记忆和同步参考；(2) Learned Rendering Engine——从共享状态为任意请求的相机生成独立且一致的视图。

Result: 在匹配的多玩家Snake基准上，相比最先进的多视角基线，MAS实现了更优的状态精度和更低的跨视角不一致性；可推进包含1024个并发玩家持续10000次循环步骤的预测世界。

Conclusion: 显式的权威状态建模为可扩展且一致的多智能体世界模拟提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Current video world models struggle in multiplayer environments because they entangle world state with view-dependent visual latents, leading to redundant compute, view inconsistencies, and poor scalability. We propose MAS (Multiplayer world models with Authoritative Shared State) to resolve this limitation. Inspired by multiplayer game architectures, MAS disentangles world dynamics and view rendering. A learned Logic Engine advances a global, authoritative typed state from joint actions without any hand-written transition function, acting as the sole recurrent memory and synchronization reference. From this shared state, a learned Rendering Engine generates independent and consistent views for any requested camera on demand. This explicit disentangling allows MAS to achieve superior state accuracy and lower cross-view inconsistency compared to state-of-the-art multi-view baselines on a matched multiplayer Snake benchmark. It advances predicted worlds with 1,024 concurrent players for 10,000 recurrent steps. Our results show that explicit, authoritative state modeling provides a practical foundation for scalable and consistent multi-agent world simulation.

</details>


### [87] [OTLesMix: Wasserstein Barycenter and Optimal Transport Map for Synthetic Lesion Generation with Diverse Shapes and Locations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06264)
*Robin Trombetta,Carole Lartizien*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OTLesMix，一种基于Wasserstein重心和最优传输计划的医学图像合成方法，用于生成多样化且真实的脑病灶样本以增强训练数据，在三个脑病灶分割任务上将Dice分数提升2.9至6.6个点，并超越现有基于混合的SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于混合策略的数据增强方法在生成病变形状和位置方面存在有限的可变性，无法充分利用增强技术提升分割模型的性能。作者希望通过最优传输理论生成更真实且更多样的合成样本。

Method: 利用Wasserstein重心和最优传输计划来结合真实样本，生成具有更高多样性和真实性的合成病变图像，用于医学图像分割的训练数据增强。

Result: 在三个脑病变分割任务上，相比无合成数据的模型，Dice分数提升了2.9至6.6个点，且优于现有的基于混合的SOTA方法。

Conclusion: OTLesMix证明了基于最优传输理论的数据合成在医学图像分割数据增强中的有效性，能够生成比现有混合方法更多样化的病变形态和位置。

Abstract: The development of deep learning over the past decade has revolutionized medical imaging segmentation, allowing the extraction of precise descriptors from large volumes to characterize pathologies. Data augmentation is a technique widely regarded as a way to improve model training. It includes simple transformations like spatial operations or intensity modifications, but also more advanced synthesis techniques. Their goal is to generate new realistic samples from an existing dataset to diversify the images used during training. Among them, several propose different mixing strategies to combine real samples. However, one of their major shortcomings is to yield limited variability in terms of generated lesion shapes and locations. In this work, we introduce a novel image synthesis method, called OTLesMix, that leverages Wasserstein barycenter and optimal transport plan to generate realistic and diverse samples. We evaluated our method on three brain lesion segmentation tasks, on which it improves the Dice score compared to a model trained without synthetic data by 2.9 to 6.6 points, and outperforms state-of-the-art mix-based methods.

</details>


### [88] [TLNM: Externally Validated Tooth Detection, Numbering and Segmentation from Smartphone Photographs Using Mask R-CNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06275)
*Arash Nedaei,Henna Tiensuu,Elina Väyrynen,Saujanya Karki,Jaakko Suutala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Mask R-CNN的牙齿定位与编号模型，利用消费级智能手机照片实现自动牙齿级解剖映射，支持远程筛查和远程牙科。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球数十亿人受口腔健康问题困扰，但专业牙科护理成本高且可及性有限。现有研究依赖临床级X光片或口内相机图像，公众无法用智能手机自行筛查。需要一种基于普通手机照片的牙齿定位与编号方案来实现预防性口腔保健。

Method: 定制Mask R-CNN管线，训练于1,272张标注的智能手机图像。包含两个领域知识驱动的机制：masked gray-world白平衡算法以缓解人工色偏，以及解剖约束检测层以强化结构有效性并抑制假阳性。评估分四阶段：内部留出测试、独立外部测试、消融研究和k-fold训练稳定性分析。

Result: 内部测试集上instance-mask AP@50为0.818，class-aware PQ为0.780，operational F1为0.884；十次运行的标准差仅0.009。外部数据集上AP@50为0.901，PQ为0.832，F1为0.928。推理管线以开源容器化API形式提供。

Conclusion: 消费级智能手机图像能够支持自动化牙齿级解剖映射，为资源受限环境中的远程筛查和远程牙科提供了可扩展、低成本的基础。该研究填补了公众自我口腔筛查的技术空白，展示了移动端牙科影像分析的可行性。

Abstract: Oral health issues affect billions globally, but the cost and limited access to professional dental care hinder preventive oral healthcare. Research relies on clinical-grade radiographs or intraoral camera images, unavailable for public self-screening. This study introduces a tooth localisation and numbering model for smartphone photographs. We developed a customised Mask Region-based Convolutional Neural Network (Mask R-CNN) pipeline trained on 1,272 annotated smartphone images. To address variability in patient-generated health data, the pipeline incorporates two domain-informed mechanisms: a masked gray-world white-balancing algorithm to mitigate artificial colour casts and an anatomically constrained detection layer to enforce structural validity and suppress false positives. Evaluation comprised four stages: internal held-out testing, independent external testing, a descriptive ablation study, and fold-based training stability analysis using the same internal test set. On the internal test set, the model achieved an instance-mask AP@50 of 0.818, class-aware PQ of 0.780, and operational F1 of 0.884. Training stability showed limited between-model variation: across ten runs, instance-mask AP@50 had a standard deviation of 0.009. On the external dataset, the model achieved an instance-mask AP@50 of 0.901, class-aware PQ of 0.832, and operational F1 of 0.928 despite differences in population, sensors, and acquisition protocols. The inference pipeline is available as an open-source, containerised API. These results demonstrate that consumer-grade smartphone imagery can support automated tooth-level anatomical mapping, offering a scalable, potentially low-cost foundation for remote screening and tele-dentistry in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [89] [UQ-Loc: Uncertainty-Aware LiDAR Scene Coordinate Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06307)
*Jacek Komorowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UQ-Loc在LightLoc架构上扩展了各向异性高斯协方差头，为基于LiDAR的场景坐标回归（SCR）提供每体素全3x3正定协方差预测，通过NLL损失和kNN空间平滑正则化训练，并用改进的SC2-PCR求解器引入不确定性加权种子评分和马氏距离内点检验，实现6-DoF定位精度提升与良好校准的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR-based SCR方法产生确定性预测，丢弃了能够提升鲁棒性和下游决策的偶然不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty），缺乏对预测不确定性的量化和评估。

Method: 在LightLoc架构上增加各向异性高斯协方差头，输出每体素的3x3正定协方差矩阵；训练采用带kNN空间平滑正则化的NLL损失；推理用改进的SC2-PCR求解器，引入不确定性加权种子评分和马氏距离内点检验；评估采用ECE指标。

Result: 实验表明UQ-Loc在6-DoF定位精度上实现了一致改进，同时产生良好校准的协方差预测。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在LiDAR SCR中显式建模偶然不确定性不仅能提供可靠的置信度估计，还能反哺于提升定位精度本身，为不确定性感知的定位方法提供了有效范式。

Abstract: LiDAR-based Scene Coordinate Regression (SCR) maps point clouds directly to 3D scene coordinates, enabling precise 6-DoF localisation without explicit map retrieval. However, existing methods produce deterministic predictions, discarding aleatoric uncertainty that could improve robustness and downstream decision-making. We present UQ-Loc, which extends the LightLoc architecture with an anisotropic Gaussian covariance head that predicts a full 3x3 positive-definite covariance matrix per voxel. Training uses a Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) loss augmented with a kNN-based spatial smoothness regulariser, while inference employs a modified SC2-PCR solver with uncertainty-weighted seed scoring and a Mahalanobis-distance inlier test. We adopt Expected Calibration Error (ECE) as a principled metric for evaluating the quality of the predicted uncertainty. Experiments demonstrate that UQ-Loc achieves consistent improvement in 6-DoF localization accuracy while producing well-calibrated covariances.

</details>


### [90] [Does FLAIR super-resolution erase or hallucinate small white-matter lesions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06311)
*Zahra Khodakarami,Yue Li,Pulkit Khandelwal,John Detre,Sandhitsu Das,Christopher Brown,David Wolk,Paul Yushkevich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了在WMH分割前应用超分辨率重建是否会影响病变内容（擦除或幻觉），使用ADNI队列的29例高分辨率FLAIR扫描，模拟3mm和5mm厚层采集，比较了多对比INR、ECLARE和三次插值三种超分辨率方法，发现主导效应是小真实病变的擦除而非幻觉，ECLARE在两种厚度下对小病变信号恢复最好，INR不优于三次插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床上FLAIR通常以厚层采集导致面内分辨率差，超分辨率常用于恢复各向同性体积，但将其应用于WMH分割前是否会擦除真实小病变或幻觉出不存在病变仍是未知问题。

Method: 使用ADNI队列29例1mm各向同性HR FLAIR扫描（专家手动标注WMH），将其降质为模拟3mm和5mm厚层采集，用多对比INR、单对比自监督模型ECLARE和三次插值上采样到HR网格，用MARS-WMH分割方法进行逐病变分析，评估检测灵敏度、擦除率和幻觉率。

Result: SR的主要影响是小真实病变的擦除而非幻觉，且随层厚增加而加剧；所有重建方法都比原始厚层扫描改善了病变检测。ECLARE在两种厚度下恢复小病变信号最佳，而INR不优于三次插值。

Conclusion: 将超分辨率应用于WMH分割前存在擦除小病变的风险，ECLARE是目前表现最好的重建方法，但所有方法仍无法完全避免小病变丢失。

Abstract: White matter hyperintensities (WMH), bright regions on Fluid-attenuated Inversion Recovery (FLAIR) scans are associated with cerebrovascular pathology and neurodegeneration. FLAIR is usually acquired with thick slices in clinical settings, giving it poor through-plane resolution. Super-resolution (SR) is a widely used method for recovering an isotropic volume from an anisotropic scan. Yet whether applying it prior to WMH segmentation preserves lesion content remains unknown: a model may erase small real lesions or hallucinate absent ones. We used 1-mm isotropic high-resolution (HR) FLAIR scans from 29 individuals in the ADNI cohort, each manually segmented for WMH by an expert. Then, we degraded each to simulated 3 and 5 mm through-plane acquisitions. Multi-contrast implicit neural representation (INR), a single-contrast self-supervised model (ECLARE), and cubic interpolation were used to upsample them onto the HR grid. WMH segmentation from a simulated thick slice and the original HR FLAIR set the floor and ceiling, respectively, for the per-lesion analysis. Of four WMH segmentation methods (WMH-SynthSeg, segcsvd, MARS-WMH, TrUE-Net), we ran the analysis under the most sensitive one to small lesions on HR (MARS-WMH) with the evaluation metrics of detection sensitivity, erasure rate (HR-detected lesions lost after reconstruction), and hallucination rate (predicted components absent from both the manual and HR segmentation). The dominant effect of SR was erasure of small real lesions, not hallucination, and it increased with slice thickness, though every reconstruction still improved lesion detection over the raw thick slice. ECLARE recovered small lesion signal best at both thicknesses, while the INR was no better than cubic interpolation.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [91] [TRACE: Learned Proprioceptive Odometry for Legged Robots under Unreliable Contact Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05975)
*Taehyeon Kong,Woojin Kim,Jemin Hwangbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE，一种用于腿式机器人在不可靠接触条件下的端到端学习型本体感知里程计估计器，通过足部感知交叉注意力模块自适应加权IMU和腿部运动学token，并结合物理启发的辅助损失、策略随机化和部分真实世界微调，在不同地形上相比经典滤波、混合和纯学习基线一致降低了位置漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人在不可靠接触条件（打滑、接触丢失）下，传统基于接触假设的里程计方法容易失败，而现有学习方法的鲁棒性不足，缺乏对接触条件自适应权重机制的有效设计。

Method: 端到端学习框架，输入为IMU和关节测量的历史序列；核心是foot-aware cross-attention模块，在无手动接触/滑移阈值的情况下自适应加权IMU与逐腿运动学token；训练采用直接监督加两个物理启发辅助损失（运动学一致性、腿部信息可靠利用）；模拟训练加入策略随机化以减少策略特定过拟合，并随后对时间编码器和预测头进行部分真实世界微调。

Result: 在多样室内外地形上，相比经典滤波法、混合法和纯学习基线，位置漂移一致降低；消融实验验证了训练目标、策略随机化和真实世界微调在不可靠接触和仿真到现实不匹配情况下的贡献。

Conclusion: TRACE通过自适应接触感知的注意力机制和物理启发训练策略，显著提升了腿式机器人在不可靠接触条件下的本体感知里程计精度和鲁棒性，并改善了sim-to-real迁移能力。

Abstract: In this paper, we present TRACE (Tokenized Robust Attention for Contact-Aware Estimation), an end-to-end learned proprioceptive odometry estimator for legged robots under unreliable contact conditions. The proposed estimator directly predicts relative displacement, relative rotation, and body-frame velocity from a recent history of onboard inertial and joint measurements. To improve robustness under unreliable contact conditions, we introduce a foot-aware cross-attention module that adaptively weights IMU and leg-wise kinematic tokens without relying on manually defined contact or slip thresholds. The estimator is trained with direct supervision and two physics-inspired auxiliary losses that promote kinematic consistency and reliable use of leg information. To reduce policy-specific overfitting and consequently improve sim-to-real transfer, simulation training incorporates policy randomization, followed by partial real-world fine-tuning of the temporal encoder and prediction head. Experiments across diverse indoor and outdoor terrains demonstrate consistent reductions in position drift compared with classical filtering-based, hybrid, and purely learning-based baselines. Ablation studies further validate the contributions of the proposed training objectives, policy randomization, and real-world fine-tuning, particularly under unreliable contacts and sim-to-real mismatch.

</details>


### [92] [Beyond Flat Policies: Hierarchical Post-Training for Embodied Agents in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05999)
*He Kong,Zengjue Chen,Qi Wang,Qianli Xing,Runliang Niu,Peidong Liu,Jiawei Li,Shiqi Wang,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiRoC 提出一种用于VLA模型的分层后训练框架，将高层任务规划与低层动作执行解耦，通过强化学习优化底层执行器，并通过对齐机制缓解规划与执行间的分布不匹配，在多个机器人操作基准上取得显著优于强基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA后训练方法将模型优化为扁平策略，难以显式建模任务进度并实现鲁棒的长期操作；分层方法虽引入任务分解但依赖离线示范的有监督学习，无法通过在线交互有效提升执行能力。

Method: HiRoC包含两个核心模块：高层规划器将复杂任务分解为可执行的子目标提供语义引导；底层执行器通过强化学习持续改进子目标条件下的动作生成。在RL前先对齐执行器与规划器生成的子目标，缓解规划与执行之间的分布不匹配。

Result: 在多个多样化机器人操作基准上，HiRoC一致优于强基线方法。综合分析进一步验证了分层后训练的有效性以及各关键组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 分层后训练框架能够有效提升VLA模型的长期操作能力，规划与执行的解耦结合RL优化与对齐机制是提升操作鲁棒性的关键。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in robotic manipulation by leveraging pretrained vision-language models. However, existing post-training methods predominantly optimize VLA models as flat policies, making it difficult to explicitly model task progression and perform robust long-horizon manipulation. Although hierarchical approaches introduce task decomposition, they mainly rely on supervised learning from offline demonstrations and cannot effectively improve execution through online interaction. To address this limitation, we propose Hierarchical Robotic Control (HiRoC), a hierarchical post-training framework that decouples high-level task planning from low-level action execution. The planner decomposes complex tasks into executable subgoals to provide explicit semantic guidance, while the executor continuously improves subgoal-conditioned action generation through reinforcement learning. To enable effective collaboration between the two modules, we further align the executor with planner-generated subgoals before reinforcement learning, mitigating the distribution misalignment between planning and execution. Extensive experiments across diverse robotic manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that HiRoC consistently outperforms strong baselines. Comprehensive analyses further validate the effectiveness of hierarchical post-training and the contribution of each key component.

</details>


### [93] [Adaptive-WAM: Quality-Guided Early-Exit Planning from Intermediate Video-Diffusion Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06008)
*Sining Ang,Yuguang Yang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Adaptive-WAM，一个基于Wan2.2-5B骨干的多出口质量感知轨迹规划器。核心洞见是：驾驶规划性能对视频扩散模型的噪声水平不敏感，且中间层即可解码出优质轨迹。因此规划器无需执行完整视频生成（避免迭代无分类器引导去噪和VAE解码），而是通过轻量级轨迹质量评分器动态决定推理深度，在NAVSIM上达到90.8 PDMS，在A100上实现170ms端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型虽然从视频扩散模型中继承了丰富的时空先验，但部署时仅需要一条自车轨迹，却仍需承担迭代式未来视频生成的巨大计算成本。作者提出一个更基本的问题：为了做出可靠的驾驶决策，视频扩散模型究竟需要执行多少？

Method: 1) 受控实验研究视频去噪时间步和DiT深度对规划性能的影响，发现规划对视频噪声水平不敏感，中间层即可解码强轨迹。2) 基于此观察，在Wan2.2-5B骨干上选择部分DiT块附加轨迹扩散头。3) 设计轻量级轨迹质量评分器，一旦解码的最佳轨迹满足质量阈值则终止推理；否则从缓存的隐藏状态继续到更深出口。4) 避免了迭代CFG去噪和VAE解码，并动态分配骨干深度。

Result: NAVSIM上自适应单轨迹规划器达90.8 PDMS；固定出口变体以64个提议达到92.6 PDMS。NAVSIM v2上获得89.9 EPDMS，为所比较的前视视频世界模型规划器中最佳结果。无需目标域微调即可迁移到nuScenes（0.88m平均L2误差，0.08%碰撞率）。A100上自适应路由将PDMS从90.62提升至90.79，平均170ms端到端规划延迟，比固定block-15规划器低约10%，比固定全深度规划器低47%。

Conclusion: 视频扩散模型中的大量计算对规划任务而言是冗余的，通过质量感知的多出口自适应架构，可以在保持甚至提升规划性能的同时大幅降低推理成本。代码将开源。

Abstract: Large video diffusion models provide rich spatiotemporal priors for autonomous driving, but existing world-action models often inherit the cost of iterative future-video generation even though deployment only requires an ego trajectory. We ask a more basic question: how much of a video diffusion model must be executed to make a reliable driving decision? Through a controlled study of video denoising timesteps and Diffusion Transformer (DiT) depth, we find that planning performance is largely insensitive to the tested video-noise levels, whereas strong trajectories can already be decoded from intermediate layers. Based on this observation, we introduce Adaptive-WAM, a quality-aware multi-exit planner built on a Wan2.2-5B backbone. Trajectory diffusion heads are attached to selected DiT blocks, and a lightweight trajectory-quality scorer terminates inference once the best trajectory decoded so far satisfies a quality threshold; otherwise, computation continues from the cached hidden state to a deeper exit. The deployed planner therefore avoids the iterative classifier-free denoising loop and VAE decoding required for future-video synthesis, while dynamically allocating backbone depth according to trajectory quality. On NAVSIM, the adaptive single-trajectory planner achieves 90.8 PDMS; a separate fixed-exit variant reaches 92.6 PDMS with 64 proposals. It further obtains 89.9 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, yielding the best reported results among the compared front-view video world-model planners. Without target-domain fine-tuning, Adaptive-WAM transfers to nuScenes with 0.88 m average L2 error and a 0.08\% collision rate. On an A100, adaptive routing improves PDMS from 90.62 to 90.79 while averaging 170 ms end-to-end planning latency, approximately 10\% below the 190 ms fixed block-15 planner and 47\% below the 320 ms fixed full-depth planner. Code will be released.

</details>


### [94] [Topometric Autonomous Vehicle Localization by Combining Visual Embeddings and Feed-Forward 3D Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06021)
*Eulogio Quemada-Torres,Alberto Jaenal,Francisco-Angel Moreno,Javier Gonzalez-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个拓扑度量(topometric)框架，将概率视觉位置识别(VPR)与前馈神经网络3D几何(FF3D)模型的度量姿态估计相结合，用于顺序外观感知定位。通过离线自动建图工具建模姿态-外观交互，在线粒子滤波融合里程计与位置信念进行FF3D推理，在三个公开基准上显著优于现有基于外观的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉定位(VL)需要同时满足紧凑性(可扩展)、外观变化鲁棒性和度量精度三个要求。VPR通过低维嵌入满足前两者但度量精度不足，标准VL方法(局部特征/神经表示)精度高但不够紧凑。作者希望通过将VPR与FF3D度量估计结合，弥补VPR精度不足的缺陷。

Method: 1) 离线阶段：自动建图工具建模场景中不同区域的拓扑度量姿态-外观交互关系；2) 在线阶段：粒子滤波从里程计和地点信念估计姿态，用于触发FF3D推理进行度量姿态估计；3) 模块化设计：描述符提取器和FF3D模型均可互换；4) 在受控图像集上迭代组合概率VPR与FF3D度量估计。

Result: 在三个已知基准上进行了广泛评估，显著优于现有基于外观的定位方法。额外分析表明，顺序信念可以在感知混淆(perceptual aliasing)下减轻严重失败。

Conclusion: 提出了一种将VPR的鲁棒性和FF3D的度量精度有效结合的模块化框架，成功将神经度量估计整合进概率外观定位中，并通过实验结果验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Effective Visual Localization (VL) requires a map of the environment that combines compactness for efficient scalability with robustness against visual appearance changes and metric precision. Through low-dimensional image embeddings, Visual Place Recognition (VPR) is able to successfully meet the first two requirements, but its low metric accuracy makes it less suitable than standard VL approaches based on local features or neural representations. This limitation can be overcome by integrating VPR with the accurate local trajectory estimates produced by feed-forward neural 3D geometry (FF3D) models. In this paper, we address sequential appearance-based localization through a topometric framework that iteratively combines probabilistic VPR with FF3D metric pose estimation in controlled image sets. Our approach proposes an automatic offline mapping tool that models the topometric pose-appearance interaction in the different parts of the scene. This map is later employed by an online particle filter that estimates the pose from odometry and belief over places for FF3D inference, successfully incorporating neural metric estimation into probabilistic appearance-based localization. We extensively evaluate the framework on three known benchmarks, demonstrating substantial improvements over existing appearance-based methods. The modularity of our approach allows the descriptor extractor and FF3D model to remain interchangeable, and a focused analysis further shows that sequential belief can mitigate severe failures under perceptual aliasing.

</details>


### [95] [IcFuzz: Fuzzing Isaac Sim with Semantic Stage Guidance and Multi-level Mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06088)
*Zhixiang Chen,Zhuangbin Chen,Ruoxi Jia,Zeqin Liao,Wei Li,Jinyang Liu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IcFuzz是首个针对NVIDIA Isaac Sim模拟器的模糊测试（fuzzing）方法。它利用LLM进行语义阶段分割以捕获上下文感知的对象语义，设计多层级变异算子系统性地测试模拟器，并使用多臂老虎机算法自适应调度变异算子以高效探索模拟状态空间。实验表明IcFuzz的代码覆盖率约为基线的190%-205%，检测到平均3.7个独特崩溃（基线为0），并在四个月内发现11个bug，其中9个已被开发者确认或修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Isaac Sim作为具身AI的GPU加速物理仿真基础设施，其固有复杂性不可避免地引入软件bug，影响仿真可靠性。现有模糊测试方法因上下文感知对象语义、层级化仿真控制和巨大仿真状态空间等挑战，难以有效测试Isaac Sim。

Method: IcFuzz包含三个核心组件：(1) LLM-based语义阶段分割，将仿真程序分解为结构化阶段以捕获上下文感知的对象语义；(2) 多层级变异算子，在层次化粒度上系统性测试模拟器（涵盖对象级、阶段级等多粒度变异）；(3) 多臂老虎机算法，自适应地调度变异算子以高效导航庞大的仿真状态空间。

Result: IcFuzz在代码覆盖率方面达到基线的约190%-205%，在三轮12小时测试中平均检测到3.7个独特崩溃（基线为0）。在实际部署约四个月期间发现11个bug，其中9个已被开发者确认或修复。

Conclusion: IcFuzz证明了结合LLM语义理解和强化学习式调度策略可以有效应对复杂机器人模拟器的模糊测试挑战，为该领域提供了首个可行的测试方案，并展示了实际bug发现能力。

Abstract: Robotics simulators serve as a foundational infrastructure for embodied AI, facilitating safe and scalable robotic system development. NVIDIA Isaac Sim has emerged as one of the most popular simulators, distinguished by its GPU-accelerated physics engine and photorealistic rendering, which enable high-fidelity modeling of complex environments. However, its inherent complexity inevitably introduces software bugs that can compromise simulation reliability. Existing fuzzing approaches struggle to test Isaac Sim effectively due to challenges of context-aware object semantics, hierarchical simulation control, and a vast simulation state space.
  In this paper, we propose IcFuzz, the first fuzzing approach for Isaac Sim. IcFuzz first performs an LLM-based semantic stage segmentation, decomposing simulation programs into structured stages that capture context-aware object semantics. Guided by this information, IcFuzz designs multi-level mutation operators to systematically exercise the simulator across hierarchical granularities. To efficiently navigate the vast simulation state space, IcFuzz employs a multi-armed bandit algorithm to adaptively schedule mutation operators. Experimental results show that IcFuzz outperforms the baselines in terms of both code coverage and bug detection. Specifically, IcFuzz achieves approximately 190\%--205\% of the code coverage of the baselines and detects an average of 3.7 unique crashes over three rounds of 12-hour tests, while no crashes are detected by the baselines. Moreover, IcFuzz has uncovered 11 bugs over approximately four months, 9 of which have been confirmed or fixed by the developers.

</details>


### [96] [Visual Grounding in Zero-Shot Vision-Language Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06154)
*J. de Curtò,Dayani Plasencia,Diego Sánchez,I. de Zarzà*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性输入消融测试（盲图、重复输入、镜面反射等）评估VLM作为零样本控制器时是否真正基于视觉输入进行决策。结果显示直接控制模式普遍失败（多数模型图像不变或近恒定输出），但提出了一种泄漏控制的对称一致性守护机制，在272帧测试上达到0.954平衡准确率，支持将VLM定位为有界的选择性危险辅助器而非整体零样本控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLM被广泛用作零样本控制器，但成功的轨迹可能仅仅来自模拟器动态或保守动作先验，而非真正的视觉理解。作者希望区分"看起来有效"与"真正基于视觉感知的决策"，揭示VLM作为控制器的真实能力边界。

Method: 设计了四类输入消融实验：盲图控制、重复相同输入、车道轴反射、非视觉基线和流程完整性检查。评估9个直接动作模型、6个结构化局部VLM和一个VLM-MPC层级结构，共32,874次评分调用，跨两个载体和三个模拟器。并提出一个基于泄漏控制对称一致性的守护机制（symmetry-consensus guardian），从16帧校准中选择模型并冻结2-of-4危险投票。

Result: 直接控制结果整体消极：恒定SLOW策略超过脚本几何控制器；多数模型对图像不敏感或近恒定输出；能识别纵向危险的模型也无法在镜面反射下正确转换左右方向。图像专用确定性正对照达到0.090m MAE和精确镜面等变性，确认刺激包含足够视觉信息。守护机制在272帧上达到0.954平衡准确率（95% CI [0.895,0.990]），弃权时承诺准确率0.973（覆盖0.824）。离线模块化重放达到0.934动作一致性和精确镜面等变。

Conclusion: 当前VLM（包括GPT-4o级模型）无法作为可靠的零样本端到端控制器，但可以在受限场景中作为有选择性的危险辅助模块使用。失败是模块化而非普遍性的，需要保留确定性感知的横向控制权。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as zero-shot controllers, but successful trajectories do not necessarily show that decisions are grounded in visual input: simulator dynamics and conservative action priors can produce favourable scores without meaningful perception. We investigate this with an input-ablation battery: blind-image controls, repeated identical inputs, lane-axis reflection, non-visual baselines, and pipeline-integrity checks. Across nine direct-action models, six structured local VLMs, and an exploratory VLM-MPC hierarchy, we analyse 32,874 scored calls over two embodiments and three simulators. The direct-control results are largely negative: a constant-SLOW policy outperforms a scripted geometric controller, several models are image-invariant or nearly constant, and models that recognize longitudinal hazards still fail to transform LEFT and RIGHT under reflection. No local VLM meets the joint longitudinal and lateral grounding criteria. However, an image-only deterministic positive control estimates the lead gap with 0.090 m MAE and exact mirror equivariance, confirming the stimuli carry sufficient visual information; the failures are modular, not universal. A post-hoc, leakage-controlled symmetry-consensus guardian selects two models from 16 calibration frames and freezes a 2-of-4 hazard vote across original and reflected views. On 272 held-out frames it reaches 0.954 balanced accuracy (episode-cluster bootstrap 95% CI [0.895,0.990]); nested leave-one-episode-out recovers the same pair and threshold in all 12 folds. Abstaining on ties raises committed balanced accuracy to 0.973 at 0.824 coverage. With deterministic perception retaining lateral authority, offline modular replay achieves 0.934 action agreement and exact mirror equivariance. These results support current VLMs as bounded, selective hazard assistants, not monolithic zero-shot controllers.

</details>


### [97] [Prior-SG: Task and Prior Driven Region Segmentation for Scene Graphs in Arbitrarily-Structured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06170)
*Giorgio Tonetti,Laurent Kneip,Abel Gawel,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Prior-SG提出了一种任务与先验驱动的分层3D场景图生成框架，将场景图构建转化为概率对齐问题。系统通过多尺度开放词汇特征融合将RGB-D流聚合为实例图，再利用LLM动态合成的先验图（环境逻辑期望）通过MAP估计推断高层功能语义，最终通过MRF优化融合视觉、几何与离散物体专家信息，在无墙开放空间环境中也能实现鲁棒的语义区域分割与零样本任务驱动空间重组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图提取框架依赖局部视觉聚类或严格几何启发式（如墙体分隔房间），在开放平面或不规则结构中表现不佳。需要一个能适应任意环境结构、并能根据高层任务动态调整空间划分的场景图生成方法。

Method: 1) 多尺度开放词汇特征融合策略将RGB-D流聚合为物理有根的Instance Graph；2) 利用LLM动态生成Prior Graph（环境的逻辑期望结构和任务相关词汇）；3) 通过MAP估计推断高层功能语义；4) 优化融合视觉、几何、离散物体专家的Markov Random Field来消解局部感知歧义。

Result: 在多样模拟住宅数据集和大规模开放平面真实环境中的验证表明：语义区域分割准确率达到SOTA，能在无物理墙体时鲁棒地划分远距离功能边界，并提供零样本本体灵活性，使机器人能够根据给定高层任务完全重构空间划分。

Conclusion: Prior-SG证明了任务/先验驱动的概率对齐方法能够克服几何启发式在开放环境中的局限，使场景图生成具有自适应性和任务感知能力，为高层空间推理和机器人导航提供了新范式。

Abstract: Hierarchical 3D scene graphs are a promising representation for high-level spatial reasoning in autonomous mobile platforms. However, existing extraction frameworks typically rely on purely local visual clustering or strict geometric heuristics, such as wall-separated rooms, which fail in open-plan or arbitrarily-structured environments. We propose Prior-SG, a task- and prior-driven framework that casts scene graph generation fundamentally as a probabilistic alignment problem. As the robot explores, it continuously aggregates an incoming RGB-D sensor stream into a physically grounded Instance Graph utilizing a multi-scale, open-vocabulary feature fusion strategy. The system then infers the high-level functional semantics of this map through a Maximum A Posteriori (MAP) estimate, guided by a Prior Graph-a logical expectation of the environment's structure and task-relevant vocabulary synthesized dynamically by a Large Language Model. By optimizing a Markov Random Field that fuses heterogeneous experts (visual, geometric, and discrete objects) with these topological priors, the system resolves local perceptual ambiguities. We validate this approach across diverse simulated residential datasets and large, open-plan real-world environments. Prior-SG achieves state-of-the-art semantic region segmentation accuracy compared to recent baselines, robustly delineates distant functional boundaries in the absence of physical walls, and uniquely provides zero-shot ontological flexibility, enabling the robot to entirely restructure its spatial partitioning based on a given high-level task.

</details>


### [98] [ErgoSurf: Ergodic Control for the Coverage of Unknown Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06208)
*Stefan Schneyer,Timo Bachmann,Maged Iskandar,Korbinian Nottensteiner,Alin Albu-Schäffer,Freek Stulp,João Silvério*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在线遍历控制（ergodic control）框架，通过内蕴触觉感知和高斯过程隐式曲面（GPIS）在任务执行过程中同时实现复杂曲面的系统覆盖与未知几何重建，并在仿真和实物机器人上验证了误差接近真值的重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遍历控制方法依赖先验的曲面几何知识或需要视觉传感器预先扫描几何信息，这些假设在未知或动态的真实环境中难以满足，限制了接触类任务（如检测、清洁、打磨、抛光）的机器人部署。

Method: 1) 使用GPIS从内蕴触觉传感在线学习全局曲面几何；2) 用接触点处的切平面点云局部近似曲面并迭代拟合GP，该近似同时作为目标分布和覆盖分布的采样域；3) 采用热扩散类比计算势场，引导遍历探索并生成平滑机器人轨迹。

Result: 通过仿真和实物机器人实验验证了同时进行遍历覆盖和在线曲面几何学习的能力，重建误差接近真值。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了在无先验几何知识、仅依赖触觉传感的情况下，机器人可以在覆盖曲面的同时准确重建未知几何，增强了遍历控制方法在真实未知环境中的实用性。

Abstract: Contact-centric tasks on surfaces, ranging from inspection and cleaning to sanding and polishing, require robots to systematically cover the surface while maintaining stable contact. Ergodic control generates trajectories that spend time at a location proportional to a desired, task-specific spatial distribution, enabling efficient information gathering and coverage. However, traditional ergodic control methods rely on prior knowledge of surface geometry or require a vision sensory input to scan the geometry beforehand, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios with unknown or dynamic environments. This paper introduces a novel online ergodic control framework that achieves systematic surface coverage while simultaneously reconstructing unknown surface geometry. We employ a Gaussian Process Implicit Surface (GPIS) model that learns global surface geometry from intrinsic tactile sensing during execution. For efficient online planning, we approximate the surface locally using point clouds sampled from tangent planes at observed contact points and iteratively fit them to the Gaussian Process. This approximation simultaneously serves as the sampling domain for both the target and the coverage distributions. We employ a heat-diffusion analogy to compute potential fields that guide ergodic exploration, translating spatial coverage objectives into smooth robot trajectories. We demonstrate our framework through simulation and real-robot experiments, validating simultaneous ergodic coverage and online surface geometry learning with reconstruction errors approaching the ground truth.

</details>


### [99] [VIDP: Variable Impedance Diffusion Policy for Compliant Robot Manipulation from Diverse Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06210)
*Hisham Khalil,Neil Fernandes,Thomas M. Kwok,Hsiu-Chin Lin,Yue Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VIDP（Variable Impedance Diffusion Policy），一种基于模仿学习的可变阻抗控制框架，利用任务参数化方向感知混合模型（TP-DAMM）从多样化示教中提取物理一致的轨迹分布，并映射为刚度分布，实现无需力传感器即可联合预测位姿动作与任务顺应性。真实机器人实验表明其在任务成功率、交互力和跟踪误差上均优于固定阻抗基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务需要精确跟踪与机械顺应性，可变阻抗控制能提升鲁棒性，但静态顺应性无法适应变化的接触约束。从示教中学习可变阻抗技能可避免复杂建模，然而顺应性在无力觉的运动学数据中是隐藏变量，现有方法从轨迹变化推断顺应性时可能将几何适应误判为有意顺应，尤其在空间布局变化时存在问题。

Method: 提出VIDP框架：1）利用Task-Parameterized Directionality-Aware Mixture Model (TP-DAMM)从多样化示教中提取物理一致的轨迹分布；2）将提取的分布映射为刚度分布（stiffness profiles）；3）通过扩散策略（diffusion policy）联合预测位姿动作和任务顺应性，无需力传感器。

Result: 真实机器人实验中，VIDP在任务成功率上显著优于固定阻抗基线；相比高刚度控制器降低交互力，相比低刚度基线降低跟踪误差，验证了可变压印控制的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过从示教数据中提取物理一致的轨迹分布并映射为刚度，VIDP实现了无需力传感器的可变阻抗控制，在接触丰富操作任务中同时兼顾精确跟踪和顺应性，优于固定阻抗方法。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires precise tracking and mechanical compliance, where variable impedance control can improve robustness in task success, whereas static compliance cannot adapt to varying contact constraints. Variable impedance skills can be learned from demonstrations, avoiding complex modeling, but compliance is a hidden variable in force-agnostic kinematic data. While existing methods infer compliance from trajectory variations, these variations may reflect geometric adaptation and not intentional compliance when subject to changing spatial layouts. Therefore, this letter introduces Variable Impedance Diffusion Policy (VIDP), an imitation learning-based variable impedance control framework leveraging a Task-Parameterized Directionality-Aware Mixture Model (TP-DAMM) to extract physically consistent trajectory distributions from diverse demonstrations. By mapping distributions to stiffness profiles, VIDP jointly predicts pose actions and task compliance without force sensors. Real-world experiments show that VIDP significantly outperforms fixed-impedance baselines in task success rate while reducing interaction forces with respect to high stiffness controllers and tracking errors with respect to low stiffness baselines.

</details>


### [100] [Design and Evaluation of a Touchscreen-Based Teleoperation Interface for Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06219)
*Juan José García Cárdenas,Alperen Kenan,Hamidreza Raei,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani,Arash Ajoudani,Adriana Tapus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究设计并评估了一种新颖的触摸屏遥操作接口，将连续手指运动直接映射到机械臂运动，实现更精细的速度控制和视觉-控制集成。与20名参与者的对比用户研究中，触摸屏相比传统操纵杆在表面接触任务中实现了53.5%的时间缩减（中位数2.50 vs 5.38分钟）、更高的路径覆盖率（90.7% vs 84.1%）、更低的过冲量，并将NASA-TLX认知负荷从52降至43（-17.3%），而单键自主模式进一步将认知负荷降至31。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核工业等挑战性环境中的表面接触任务（如擦拭采样）需要精确的路径/力跟踪和避障，传统操纵杆接口难以有效支持这些需要持续注意力的精细操作，因此需要更直观、更自然的遥操作界面。

Method: 设计了一种触摸屏遥操作接口，将手指连续运动直接映射为机械臂运动，提供精细速度控制并集成控制与可视化。使用Franka Emika Panda臂在跨国家远程环境中进行模拟真实表面操作的对比用户研究，采集运动学、生理和行为数据，从任务性能、认知负荷和操作者信任三个维度评估触摸屏、传统操纵杆和单键自主模式三种控制条件。

Result: 触摸屏接口显著优于操纵杆：任务完成时间中位数从5.38分钟降至2.50分钟（-53.5%），正弦路径覆盖率从84.1%提升至90.7%，两种路径几何的过冲量均更低。NASA-TLX认知负荷从52降至43（-17.3%），单键自主模式最低为31（-40.4% vs 操纵杆）。

Conclusion: 该触摸屏接口易于实现，能显著提升遥操作表面任务的效率和准确性，同时降低操作者认知负荷，为核工业等领域的表面操作提供了优于传统操纵杆的遥操作方案。

Abstract: Intuitive teleoperation interfaces are crucial for the safe and effective operation of robotic manipulators in challenging environments. In the nuclear industry, surface contact tasks such as swab sampling require precise path and force tracking, obstacle avoidance, and sustained operator attention, which conventional joystick interfaces struggle to support effectively.
  This study designs and evaluates a novel touchscreen teleoperation interface that maps continuous finger movements directly to robotic manipulator motions, provides finer velocity control, and integrates control with visualization, enabling more natural, precise, and intuitive surface interaction than conventional controllers. A comparative user study with 20 participants evaluated task performance and workload using the proposed touchscreen, a conventional joystick, and a single-click autonomous mode. Tasks simulated realistic surface manipulation using a Franka Emika Panda arm, remotely controlled from another country. Kinematic, physiological, and behavioral data were recorded to comprehensively assess task performance, cognitive load, and operator trust across each control condition.
  Participants completed teleoperation tasks more efficiently and accurately with the touchscreen interface, achieving a 53.5% reduction in completion time (median: 2.50 vs. 5.38 min), higher in-area coverage on the sinusoidal path (90.7% vs. 84.1%), and lower overshoot on both path geometries compared with the joystick. Cognitive load, quantified via NASA-TLX (0-100), decreased from joystick to touchscreen (mean TLX 52 to 43; -9 points, -17.3%) and was lowest under the autonomous one-click mode (31; -21 points vs. joystick, -40.4%; -12 vs. touchscreen, -27.9%). This research presents an easy-to-implement touchscreen interface that improves performance in teleoperated surface tasks while reducing cognitive load.

</details>


### [101] [Robot Learning from Human Demonstrations: Handwritten Alphabet Trajectories and Human-Likeness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06221)
*Alperen Kenan,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从演示学习(LfD)的框架，用于生成类人机器人运动。通过触摸屏遥操作界面从22名参与者收集了3,142个手写演示（覆盖52个拉丁字母大小写组合），在标准GMM/GMR基础上扩展了力和归一化时间维度，并适配了非连续多段轨迹以支持跨演示泛化。21名参与者的用户研究显示生成轨迹的类人评分为71.50/100（SD=22.56），参与者认为几何定位和轨迹序列是影响类人感知的最关键因素。数据已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人机器人运动被认为是建立信任和实现自然人机协作的关键，但传统显式编程难以教授技能。LfD提供了一种发展性框架，使机器人能通过观察和模仿人类动态来发展运动技能，本文旨在构建一个完整且可复现的LfD框架，学习类人运动并量化验证其类人感知。

Method: 框架包含三部分：(1) 数据收集——通过触摸屏遥操作界面收集22名参与者的手写轨迹数据，涵盖位置、接触力和时间；(2) 轨迹学习——在标准GMM/GMR方法上引入力和归一化时间维度，并扩展以处理非连续多段轨迹，实现跨演示泛化；(3) 感知评估——21名参与者用连续量表对生成轨迹的类人程度进行0-100评分（50为中性点）。

Result: 生成轨迹的整体类人评分为71.50（SD=22.56），多数轨迹被认为更接近人类而非机器人。参与者识别出几何定位和轨迹序列是最具影响力的感知因素，并对类人机器人行为持积极态度。数据集已作为开源基准发布。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地从演示中学习并生成了被感知为类人的机器人运动，通过引入力和时间维度并处理多段轨迹提升了标准GMM/GMR方法的表现。开源数据集为类人机器人运动方法的开发和评估提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration (LfD) provides a developmental framework through which robots can develop motor skills by observing and imitating human dynamics, reducing reliance on explicit programming to teach a skill to a robot. The resulting human-like robot motion is recognised as a key factor in building trust and enabling natural collaboration in human-robot interaction. This paper presents a framework for learning human-like robot motion from demonstration, including data collection, probabilistic trajectory learning, and perceptual user evaluation. A dataset of 3,142 handwriting demonstrations was collected from 22 participants across all 52 Latin alphabet character-case combinations via a touchscreen teleoperation interface, capturing planar position, contact force, and timing.
